**[00:20:08.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000049)  
Good morning to our colleagues in government, our resource persons, and everyone present today.

**[00:20:13.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000050)  
Today, we deliberate on the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of Public Works and Highways,

**[00:20:20.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000051)  
one of the government's most important implementing agencies.

**[00:20:24.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000052)  
The DPWH plays a crucial role not only in building roads, bridges, flood control systems,

**[00:20:29.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000053)  
and other public

**[00:20:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000054)  
infrastructure,

**[00:20:30.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000055)  
but also driving economic growth.

**[00:20:34.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000056)  
Infrastructure spending has a significant multiplier effect on the economy,

**[00:20:39.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000057)  
creating jobs, improving connectivity, attracting investments, and stimulating economic activity.

**[00:20:47.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000058)

**[00:20:54.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000059)  
opening up opportunities, and ultimately improving the quality of lives of our people.

**[00:21:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000060)  
During the recent DBCC briefing,

**[00:21:03.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000061)  
DEPDev reported that infrastructure spending averaged around 5.8% of GDP from 2022 to 2024,

**[00:21:12.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000062)  
but dropped significantly to around 4.7% in 2025.

**[00:21:19.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000063)  
And at present, 2026 is much even worse.

**[00:21:24.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000064)  
Second quarter is only 2% GDP.

**[00:21:27.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000065)  
DepDEP also identified,

**[00:21:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000066)  
underspending and delays in project implementation as risks to achieving our growth targets.

**[00:21:36.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000067)  
This comes amid a sharp 32.2% contraction in public construction for the first half of 2026,

**[00:21:44.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000068)  
raising serious concerns about the pace of infrastructure development and its impact on economic growth.

**[00:21:52.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000069)  
Noong nakaraang taon, naging sentro ng kontrobersya ang DPWH dahil sa mga anomalya sa flood control projects.

**[00:22:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000070)  
It is one of, if not the largest, corruption scandal in the history of our country.

**[00:22:06.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000071)

**[00:22:14.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000072)

**[00:22:20.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000073)

**[00:22:26.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000074)

**[00:22:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000075)

**[00:22:32.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000076)

**[00:41:54.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000521)

**[00:42:38.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000543)  
So, that po,

Ito na po, yung budget reforms.

yung tinatawag na

**[00:43:13.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000559)

**[00:43:16.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000560)

**[00:43:17.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000561)

**[01:06:47.240] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001136)  
Good morning, Mr. Chair, Senate President, Ma'am.

**[01:06:53.000] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001137)  
Magandang umaga po.

**[01:06:54.440] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001138)  
For the Pasig-Marikina River Channel Improvement Project,

**[01:06:57.100] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001139)  
this is the last Pasig-Marikina stages out of the five packages that we had actually proposed

**[01:07:04.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001140)  
for undertaking within Metro Manila area,

**[01:07:08.580] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001141)  
covering the river from Pasig River all the way to Pasig.

**[01:07:13.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001142)  
That's the Marikina River.

**[01:07:14.760] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001143)  
The projects here embodied the constructions of about six kilometers,

**[01:07:20.560] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001144)  
closing the gap of the river channel improvement works downstream of Rosario

**[01:07:24.600] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001145)  
all the way to Santo Nino,

**[01:07:26.780] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001146)  
including the constructions of the Marikina control gate structures

**[01:07:30.020] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001147)  
that would regulate the flood water coming down from Marikina River to Pasig River down to Manila Bay.

**[01:07:36.980] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001148)  
So, the constructions of these projects will be fully completed in 2030,

**[01:07:43.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001149)  
And after these completions, there's one more lacking Phase 6, we call it Phase 6, which is the most ultimate one, that is actually the constructions of the two dams in the upper Marikina River Channel, which will now regulate the flood water of 50 million cubic meters on the top of Mount Oro, and constructions of four retarding basins, and the completions of the entire 35 kilometers channel improvement for Pasig.

**[01:08:13.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001150)  
Marikina River Channel. And this will also complement, Mr. Chair, to add to the functionality of the Pasig-Marikina entire river basin, the upcoming detailed design works for next year of the Para?aque Spillway. The spillway will address the regulated flow of floodwater that goes through Manila to Laguna de Bay and discharge big volumes of floodwater to Manila Bay through the nine-kilometer Para?aque underground tunnel.

**[01:08:44.500] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001151)  
So the projects here, for these particular projects, once this is completed, and with that Paranaque Spillway complementary, it will bring down the flood water elevations of Pasig River by half full bank capacity, and eventually will allow large volumes of flooding from the inland cities of Metro Manila, particularly Quezon City, Manila, San Juan, and part of Makatiang Mandaluyong.

**[01:09:10.080] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001152)  
So with that very low elevations of flood water,

**[01:09:13.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001153)  
Pasig River, it can absorb more flood water from the inland side, and that can deduce drastically large volumes of water in the inland, kasi karamihan po niyan, ang cost talagang ng baha, especially from Marikina sections, pag full blast ang baba niya, it will actually inundate in the downstream stretch of Pasig River.

**[01:09:33.280] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001154)  
Because of the high flow of Pasig River, the water that comes from San Juan River has been actually stopped at the confluence.

**[01:09:42.040] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001155)  
So nagba-backflow po siya pataas.

**[01:09:43.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001156)  
So with this completion of this project, Mr. Chair, this can be a big relief for flooding of Metro Manila, although we are not telling na ito po ang solution, but because we are also embarking with Secretary's guidance to work also on the drainage master plan of the 17 cities for Metro Manila.

**[01:10:10.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001157)  
Kasi yun na lang po yung nakikita natin, yung 17 cities.

**[01:10:13.360] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001158)  
Na drainage master plan nila, karamihan po dito because of the rapid urbanization, maliit na lang, o kaya minsan barado, and we have to look more deeper on that, where ADB, JICA, and World Bank are also helping us to come up with this study.

**[01:10:27.140] DPWH Senior Undersecretary Emil K. Sadain** (asr-001159)  
Itong November po, matatapos yung study na nagagawa po.

**[01:19:30.420] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001361)  
under RA 12076,

**[01:19:33.460] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001362)  
the NDRRMC

**[01:19:34.500] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001363)  
through the Office of the Civil Defense

**[01:19:36.620] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001364)  
act as the lead agency in the

**[01:19:38.520] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001365)

**[01:19:40.200] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001366)

**[01:19:42.500] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001367)  
Ligtas Pinoy Centers.

**[01:19:44.380] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001368)  
DPWH is the primary

**[01:19:46.460] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001369)  
agency for

**[01:19:48.800] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001370)  
the construction of these LPCs.

**[01:19:51.240] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001371)  
So for 2027,

**[01:19:52.600] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001372)  
we're proposing two Ligtas Pinoy

**[01:19:54.320] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001373)  
Centers for each region.

**[01:19:56.800] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001374)  
Based on the

**[01:19:58.300] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001375)  
released five-year implementation

**[01:20:00.720] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001376)  
plan of NDRRMC.

**[01:20:03.460] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001377)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:20:05.060] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001378)  
Based on the five-year

**[01:20:06.640] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001379)

**[01:20:08.280] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001380)  
75 million is allotted

**[01:20:10.920] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001381)

**[01:20:12.380] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001382)

**[01:20:15.400] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001383)

**[01:20:20.420] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-001386)

**[02:04:49.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002886)

**[02:04:53.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002887)

**[02:05:02.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002889)

**[02:05:19.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002893)

**[02:05:23.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002895)

**[02:05:46.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002902)

**[02:05:51.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002904)

**[02:05:54.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002906)

**[02:05:56.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002907)  
So, pagka po meron dito na talagang makikita naman,

**[02:06:01.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002908)

**[02:06:03.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002909)

**[02:06:04.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002910)

**[02:06:07.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002911)

**[02:06:11.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002912)

**[02:06:17.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002915)

**[02:06:18.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002916)  
Although, I would like to believe na

**[02:06:20.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002917)

**[02:06:23.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002918)

**[02:06:26.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002920)

**[02:06:28.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002921)

**[02:06:29.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002922)

**[02:06:32.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002923)

**[02:06:43.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002928)

**[02:06:43.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002929)

**[02:06:46.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002930)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[02:06:48.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002931)  
Mr. Senate President,

**[02:06:49.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002932)

**[02:06:50.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002933)

**[02:07:17.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002943)

**[02:44:48.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004111)

**[02:44:50.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004112)  
So my question there is

**[02:44:51.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004113)

**[02:44:56.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004115)

**[02:44:58.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004116)  
much is going to urban?

**[02:45:00.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004117)  
If you do, I'd like to hear it. If not, can you have it computed?

**[02:45:04.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004118)  
I mean, you are absolutely correct, Mr. Chairman, that there's a lot of catching up to do in terms of building the appropriate infrastructure to mitigate the effects of flooding.

**[02:45:21.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004119)  
Because it's a combination of many factors and overpopulation, obviously, is one of it. As a result, garbage is another problem that also adds to it.

**[02:45:35.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004120)  
But I want to zero in on one important point that you made, which is I think we really need to take a hard look at the building code.

**[02:45:42.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004121)  
And I'm very happy for that. The President and Congress has already made this a priority.

**[02:45:48.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004122)  
Kasi po talaga, we really need to be very strict

**[02:45:51.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004123)  
in enforcing no-build zones along waterways. I think if there is one major intervention po that needs to be done, and it is urgent, is to reimpose the no-build zones along waterways.

**[02:46:10.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004124)  
It is not easy at all, especially in Metro Manila, kasi nga po mahirap po tayong magpaalis ng ating mga kababayan

**[02:46:20.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004125)  
na nandoon na nang matagal, at mayroon po sa tabi ng waterway, nasa ibabaw na po.

**[02:46:28.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004126)  
Meaning, napatungan na po yung water. So I think that is very, very important.

**[02:46:33.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004127)  
But definitely, Honorable Minority Leader, we will be working hard with you on this and how we address it.

**[02:46:41.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004128)  
But you are absolutely correct that this is not a simple solution, given the challenges that we face because of rapid urbanization.

**[02:46:49.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004129)  
I agree with you totally.

**[02:46:51.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004130)  
Especially, where are the no-building zones?

**[02:46:53.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004131)

**[02:47:00.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004132)  
Because it endangers.

**[02:47:01.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004133)  
It's the same with reclamation.

**[02:47:04.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004134)  
But that's another topic which is quite sensitive given the investments.

**[02:47:08.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004135)  
But we should really have a study, maybe, of future reclamations.

**[02:47:17.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004136)  
But when you say garbage and overpopulation,

**[02:47:21.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004137)  
you're familiar.

**[02:47:22.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004138)

**[02:47:27.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004139)

**[02:47:31.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004140)  
So, if you look at this graph,

**[02:47:32.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004141)  
overpopulation relatively.

**[02:47:45.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004142)  
Kasi ang laki ng ating bansa at ang laki ng lupa ng bansa.

**[02:47:49.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004143)  
Pero kung titignan mo,

**[02:47:49.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004144)  
overpopulation in NCR.

**[02:47:50.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004145)  
Ang NCR.

**[02:47:51.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004146)

**[02:47:56.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004147)

**[02:47:58.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004148)  
Region 2, it's 124.

**[02:48:00.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004149)  
Region 11, it's 257.

**[02:48:02.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004150)

**[02:48:04.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004151)

**[02:48:11.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004152)  
which is quite highly populated na rin,

**[02:48:14.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004153)  
ang layo, 567 and less than 1,000 compared to 21,000.

**[02:48:20.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004154)  
But that's where I think the shift,

**[02:48:22.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004155)  
and I know you believe in this because you moved,

**[02:48:24.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004156)

**[02:48:30.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004157)  
So, the thing is in Europe, for example,

**[02:48:34.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004158)

**[02:48:43.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004159)

**[02:48:46.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004160)

**[02:48:50.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004161)

**[02:48:51.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004162)  
Wala.

**[02:48:51.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004163)

**[02:48:54.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004164)

**[02:48:57.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004165)

**[02:49:02.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004166)

**[02:49:05.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004167)

**[02:49:07.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004168)

**[02:49:14.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004169)

**[02:49:16.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004170)

**[02:49:16.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004171)

**[02:49:20.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004172)

**[02:49:25.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004173)

**[02:49:27.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004174)  
And if you look at the budget, the last year of GMA, President Arroyo,

**[02:49:33.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004175)

**[02:49:36.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004176)

**[02:49:41.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004177)

**[02:49:46.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004178)

**[02:49:55.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004180)

**[02:50:01.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004181)

**[02:50:05.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004182)

**[02:50:09.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004183)

**[02:50:15.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004184)

**[02:50:27.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004187)

**[02:50:30.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004188)

**[02:50:33.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004189)

**[02:50:37.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004190)

**[02:50:46.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004192)

Urbanization.

**[02:50:49.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004194)

**[02:51:27.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004207)

**[02:51:44.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004210)

**[02:51:53.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004213)  
Kasi napakarami pang lupa dun eh.

Diba?

**[02:51:56.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004215)

**[02:52:00.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004216)

**[03:25:41.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004821)

**[03:25:41.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004822)

**[03:25:46.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004823)

**[03:25:48.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004824)

**[03:25:49.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004825)

**[03:25:50.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004826)

**[03:25:55.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004827)

**[03:25:58.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004828)

**[03:26:06.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004830)

**[03:26:12.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004831)

**[03:26:17.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004832)

**[03:26:20.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004833)

**[03:26:22.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004834)

**[03:26:24.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004835)

**[03:26:27.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004836)

**[03:26:30.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004837)

**[03:26:35.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004838)

**[03:26:35.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004839)  
So, may I propose that, Sekvins, na

**[03:26:38.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004840)  
if you cannot do a major shift to rural development in 2027,

**[03:26:44.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004841)

**[03:26:47.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004842)

**[03:26:49.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004843)

**[03:26:49.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004844)

**[03:26:52.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004845)  
Kasi if you look at the top,

**[03:26:54.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004846)  
there are about 5 to 10 provinces that are perennially,

**[03:26:58.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004847)  
for the last 20 years,

**[03:27:00.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004848)  
in the 20 poorest.

**[03:27:03.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004849)  
Mataas doon ang stunting,

**[03:27:05.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004850)  
mataas doon ang lahat, heart disease, etc.

**[03:27:08.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004851)  
Mataas doon ang insurgency.

**[03:27:10.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004852)  
So, baka hindi DOH, DOTR,

**[03:27:14.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004853)

**[03:27:16.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004854)

**[03:27:18.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004855)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[03:27:19.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004856)  
very important point,

**[03:27:20.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004857)

**[03:27:23.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004858)

**[03:27:26.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004859)

**[03:27:30.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004860)

**[03:27:31.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004861)  
in terms of poverty incidents,

**[03:27:32.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004862)  
it is really Region 5, Region 8, and Region 13.

**[03:27:39.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004863)  
And we are happy to report po that,

**[03:27:42.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004864)  
for example po, sa Region 5, sa Kabikuran,

**[03:27:46.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004865)

**[03:27:48.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004866)  
Tumaas po ito ng...

**[03:27:49.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004867)  
3 percentage points from dating 4% lang po ng budget,

**[03:27:53.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004868)  
ngayon po ay 7% na.

**[03:27:55.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004869)  
Ang Region 8 po,

**[03:27:57.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004870)  
in fact, ang Region 8 po,

**[03:27:59.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004871)  
naunahan na ang Region 7.

**[03:28:01.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004872)  
Ang Region 7 po,

**[03:28:03.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004873)  
dito sa budget natin ngayon,

**[03:28:04.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004874)

**[03:28:06.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004875)  
Ang Region 8 po,

**[03:28:07.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004876)  
ay 6.38%.

**[03:28:09.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004877)

**[03:28:12.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004878)  
So, we really made it a point po to try.

**[03:28:14.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004879)  
Obviously, we cannot do it just in one year,

**[03:28:17.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004880)  
but I think,

**[03:28:18.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004881)  
we are on the right track po

**[03:28:20.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004882)  
in terms of making sure that the

**[03:28:22.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004883)  
poorest areas get the most funds for...

**[03:28:30.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004887)

**[03:28:32.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004888)

Mr. Chairman, I'm happy to report po that San Perfecto-Batis pumping station is funded in 2027 in the NEP.

**[04:56:49.030] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006320-split-7)

**[05:45:40.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009010)  
Mr. Chairman, unang-una po, ang mandate ng Pangulo mismo is tapusin yung mga hindi tapos.

**[05:45:49.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009011)  
So ano po, gagawin po namin ang lahat kahit na po na isauli na dahil sa...

**[05:45:57.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009012)  
National Budget Circular 603 which directed all of national government agencies to return unused funds

**[05:46:04.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009013)  
to finance relief programs under the State of National Energy Emergency.

**[05:46:09.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009014)  
Ang objective pa rin po natin, kunyari po, meron pong mga multi-purpose buildings,

**[05:46:16.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009015)  
halimbawa, na hindi na tapos, mga kalye na hindi na tapos.

**[05:46:20.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009016)  
Yan po ang inatasang ko po ang mga district engineers natin na i-resubmit po para po matapos.

**[05:46:27.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009017)  
Kasi hindi naman po po pwede na nakatenga lang ang mga project na hindi tapos.

**[05:46:32.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009018)  
So we will do our best po to finish as fast as possible yung mga nakatenggang project.

**[05:46:36.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009019)

**[05:46:43.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009020)  
Pasado na ito sa Kongreso.

**[05:46:45.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009021)  
At nasagaan na po ito noong 2025.

**[05:46:49.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009022)  
At sa mga lugar rin po na tinamaan po ng Lindol, SecVins,

**[05:46:55.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009023)  
baka pwede nyo ma...

**[05:46:57.080] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009024)  
Mabalikan lang po itong mga proyektong ito na...

**[05:47:00.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009025)

**[05:58:21.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009215)

**[05:58:24.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009216)  
P.S.C.O. ng sports.

**[05:58:27.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009217)  
At sila po'y nagmamakawa na ito,

**[05:58:30.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009218)

**[05:58:34.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009219)  
yung dating Ultra, yung Field Sports, yung RMC,

**[05:58:38.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009220)  
Rizal Memorial Complex.

**[05:58:40.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009221)  
Ako nga mismo, bilang chairperson ng sports,

**[05:58:44.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009222)

**[05:58:48.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009223)

**[05:58:52.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009224)  
Habang naglalaro ka,

**[05:58:53.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009225)  
may tulo na minsan.

**[05:58:55.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009226)  
Sana po'y komportable naman po ang ating mga atleta.

**[05:58:58.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009227)  
Sila po nagbibigay sa atin ng karangalan tuwing may mga sporting events sa ating bansa.

**[05:59:03.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009228)  
Katulad ngayon sa Asian Games.

**[05:59:05.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009229)

**[05:59:08.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009230)

**[05:59:13.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009231)  
Dapat supportado sila ng gobyerno.

**[05:59:15.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009232)

**[05:59:18.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009233)

**[05:59:21.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009234)

**[05:59:25.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009235)

**[05:59:28.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009236)  
Since nasa DPWH ka, kindly po.

**[05:59:32.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009237)  
Mr. Chairman, actually, tuloy-tuloy po ang supporta namin kay Chairman Gregorio.

**[05:59:38.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009238)  
Yung pong mga repairs natin sa Rizal, sa Ultra,

**[05:59:43.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009239)

**[05:59:44.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009240)

**[05:59:47.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009241)

**[05:59:49.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009242)  
Pero tama po kayo.

**[05:59:50.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009243)  
Our athletes need more support.

**[05:59:52.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009244)  
Tututukan din po namin yan sa mga darating na buwan po.

**[05:59:57.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009245)  
Mga gago na po, marami tayo.

**[06:00:00.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009246)  
yung i-host ng mga sporting events in the coming years, in the coming months.

**[06:00:06.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009247)  
Coordinated po kami very closely with Chairman Pato

**[06:00:09.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009248)  
para po tuloy-tuloy ang paayos natin ng mga facilities.

**[06:00:12.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009249)  
Pati yung swimming pool sa UP Mindanao

**[06:00:15.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009250)  
or maaari yung soccer field rin po matulungan nyo.

**[06:00:21.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009251)  
May tanong lang po ko, Sec Vins, about itong sa budget ng DPWH.

**[06:00:25.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009252)  
Inaasan nyo ba sa PSC yung mga infrastructure

**[06:00:27.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009253)  
o meron rin kayo ang nagpapagawa?

**[06:00:32.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009254)  
Ang DBM po for this year, for 2027,

**[06:00:36.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009255)  
pinanggal po ng DBM sa DPWH yung mga convergence budgets.

**[06:00:45.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009256)  
So, kagaya po ng pinag-iwanag po kanina,

**[06:00:48.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009257)  
yung sa DOT, yung sa DTI, sa DOTR,

**[06:00:52.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009258)  
kasama na rin po yung sa PSC.

**[06:00:54.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009259)  
So, ang ano po nila, ipinasok na po nila yan

**[06:00:57.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009261)  
Sa mother agency, in this case, for sports facilities, sa PSC.

**[06:01:03.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009262)  
Pero tumituri naman po ang aming cooperation ng PSC.

**[06:01:07.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009263)  
At kung kakikanganin po nila kami sa DPWH,

**[06:01:10.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009264)  
ready po kaming tumulong sa kanila.

**[06:01:12.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009265)  
So, ibig sabihin, tinanggal sa inyong.

**[06:01:18.040] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009266)  
Sayang naman.

**[06:01:20.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009267)  
Sana manggagaling sa DPWH yung pondo.

**[06:01:24.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009268)  
Mapapakinabangan po ng mga atleta natin ito.

**[06:01:27.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009269)  
Anyway, another question na itong sa building code,

**[06:01:34.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009270)  
disaster preparedness.

**[06:01:36.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009271)  
Mr. Chair, I filed the proposed new building code

**[06:01:39.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009272)  
which aims to protect the people's lives

**[06:01:42.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009273)  
and mitigate the impact of disasters

**[06:01:44.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009274)  
by setting standards and benchmarks

**[06:01:47.480] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009275)  
that all buildings and infrastructures

**[06:01:50.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009276)  
must meet with particularly the recommended

**[06:01:53.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009277)  
structural stability and integrity

**[06:01:55.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009278)  
designed to withstand disasters

**[06:01:57.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009279)  
and to prevent the spread of natural disasters.

**[06:01:59.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009280)  
Ilang dekada na po nakalipas itong building code natin,

**[06:02:01.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009281)  
o yung national building code.

**[06:02:03.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009282)  
Naipasa po ito noong 1977.

**[06:02:05.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009283)  
Marami na po nagbago sa panahon ngayon

**[06:02:09.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009284)  
at marami na tayong natutunan tungkol sa building safety

**[06:02:11.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009285)  
dahil po sa makabagong teknolohiya or siyensya, rather.

**[06:02:17.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009286)  
Makabagong siyensya.

**[06:02:19.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009287)  
Panahon na po upang pag-aralan natin at pag-a-update po

**[06:02:21.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009288)  
ng national building code.

**[06:02:23.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009289)  
Malaki po ang gagampanan ng DPWH para masiguro na ligtas.

**[06:02:27.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009291)  
Ang ating infrastruktura.

**[06:02:31.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009292)  
Priority nyo ba ito?

**[06:02:37.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009293)  
In fact, meron po kaming current technical working group

**[06:02:41.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009294)  
na nagre-review ng buong building code.

**[06:02:43.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009295)  
Lumang-luma na po ito. Mayigit 20 taon na or

**[06:02:48.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009296)  
50 years na pala. Sorry.

**[06:02:51.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009297)  
50 years na po next year ito.

**[06:02:53.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009298)  
Kaya po dapat na po tagalain.

**[06:02:56.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009299)  
Thank you po, Sen. Bong, for your initiative na talagang i-review na ito.

**[06:03:02.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009300)  
Ang co-chairs po nung aming review committee ay si Undersecretary Conti

**[06:03:07.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009301)  
at si Undersecretary Izquibil for Technical Services.

**[06:03:10.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009302)  
Siguro po, maiksing update lang po kay, from USEC, Conti

**[06:03:15.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009303)  
and then maybe a few updates from USEC, Lara.

**[06:03:18.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009304)  
Thank you, Secretary. Magandang habon po, Sen. Bong Go.

**[06:03:22.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009305)  
Nag-issue po si Secretary Vince ng Special Order No.

**[06:03:24.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009306)  
155.

**[06:03:27.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009307)  
Talaga nga po siya na magkaroon ng review committee.

**[06:03:31.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009308)  
At para sa kaalaman po ng ating kagalanggalang ni Sen. Bong Go,

**[06:03:35.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009309)  
dalawang beses po nagkakaroon ng meeting

**[06:03:38.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009310)  
ang kada buwan ang aming committee.

**[06:03:41.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009311)  
At sa loob po ng dalawang linggo ay matatapos na po namin

**[06:03:44.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009312)  
yung aming mga proposed amendments.

**[06:03:46.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009313)  
Kasama rin po at na-consider po, Sen. Bong Go,

**[06:03:49.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009314)  
yung mga provision po doon sa inyong panukalang batas din po.

**[06:03:52.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009315)  
At target nga po namin next week, first week po ng October,

**[06:03:56.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009316)  
ay ma-finalize na po namin ang resulta ng review committee

**[06:04:01.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009317)  
dahil hinahabol nga po namin, Mr. Chairman, Sen. Bong Go,

**[06:04:05.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009318)  
na by February of 2027, that is the 50th anniversary po

**[06:04:10.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009319)  
ng ating National Building Code.

**[06:04:12.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009320)  
So sana po mahabol ito at who knows, very timely po

**[06:04:16.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009321)  
na ma-firmahan po yung batas sapagkat sinabi naman po ng ating presidente ito nung SONA,

**[06:04:21.680] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-009322)  
na itibigyan ng urgent attention po.

**[06:04:25.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009323)  
Salamat po. Tandaan niyo lang yung golden anniversary ng building code

**[06:04:30.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009324)  
para ma-update na po ito.

**[06:04:33.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009325)  
Before my last question, Sec. Vince.

**[06:04:39.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009326)  
Sen. Tom, I think si Usec Lara po. Kasi siya po yung technical director.

**[06:04:47.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009327)  
Siya po yung magaling na engineer na batang bata.

**[06:04:51.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009328)  
Ano po?

**[06:04:52.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009329)  
Sen. We just wanted to say na the Department of Public Works and Highways

**[06:04:57.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009330)  
is working with the Association of Structural Engineers of the Philippines

**[06:05:00.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009331)  
and we're updating the National Structural Code of the Philippines.

**[06:05:03.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009332)  
Nandito na po sa DP and it incorporates the updated seismic maps and the wind map.

**[06:05:08.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009333)  
So this is updating with the current conditions po

**[06:05:12.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009334)  
and the higher probability of exceedance po sa ating earthquake and typhoons.

**[06:05:16.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009335)  
So yun po yung malaking tulong po para pag nagde-design po tayo,

**[06:05:20.680] DPWH OIC Undersecretary Lara Marisse I. Esquibil** (asr-009336)  
eh yung mga recorded, highest recorded and yung mga mataas na probability na po ang nakakonsider.

**[06:05:28.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009337)  
Napansin ko talaga Usec, Sec. Vince, sa mga nasira ng lindol.

**[06:05:36.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009338)  
Yun talaga yung mga old structures para hindi na nila kinaya.

**[06:05:43.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009339)  
So doon sa Region 12, nung nakaraang lindol, yun talagang napansin natin.

**[06:05:49.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009340)  
And I'm sure mas alam niyo po yun, lalong-lalo na po yung mga engineer.

**[06:05:53.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009341)  
And before my last question or manifestation,

**[06:05:57.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009342)  
I would also like to raise the seeming inequitable regional allocation of DPWH projects.

**[06:06:05.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009343)  
Napapansin ko po kung saan po nangangailangan ng infrastruktura.

**[06:06:09.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009344)  
Yun po yata yung kukonti ang budget.

**[06:06:12.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009345)  
Hindi dapat puro sa malalaking siyudad lamang po ang proyekto.

**[06:06:15.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009346)  
Pakitsilip niyo lang po ito.

**[06:06:17.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009347)  
Mga probinsya, mga rural communities.

**[06:06:21.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009348)  
Ito pong mga geographically isolated and disadvantaged areas.

**[06:06:26.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009349)  
And particularly Mindanao and the Visayas.

**[06:06:31.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009350)  
Sana po magkaroon sila ng fair share of infrastructure investments.

**[06:06:37.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009351)  
Maalala niyo po nun, Sec. Vince, yun pong parating sinasabi ni former President Duterte.

**[06:06:45.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009352)  
Nung kakaupulang niya bilang presidente,

**[06:06:49.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009353)  
meron kasi mga gobernador na meron mga proyektong inaprobohan ng previous administration pa

**[06:06:59.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009354)  
during the time of former President Aquino.

**[06:07:03.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009355)  
So napagtungtong nung panahon ni President Duterte, nung 2016,

**[06:07:07.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009356)  
nag-alala yung mga governor. Sabi nila baka hindi na ipagpatuloy itong mga proyektong ito.

**[06:07:12.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009357)  
Dahil may lugar na talo si President Duterte.

**[06:07:15.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009358)  
Kahit sa Mindanao, alam niyo na po yun.

**[06:07:18.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009359)  
Yung isang tulayo doon na laking tulong po na iikot ka po.

**[06:07:22.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009360)  
Yung pang...

**[06:07:24.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009361)  
How do you call that? Pangil?

**[06:07:26.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009362)  
Pangil or pangil?

**[06:07:28.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009363)  
Pangil.

**[06:07:29.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009364)  
Pangil.

**[06:07:33.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009365)  
Pangil Bay, di ba?

**[06:07:34.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009366)  
Maalala niyo, laking tulong po itong proyektong ito.

**[06:07:38.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009367)  
From 3 hours na iikot ka, Osamis going to Lanao del Norte.

**[06:07:44.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009368)  
Ngayon po, 15 minutes na lang po.

**[06:07:46.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009369)  
So nag-worry yung mga opisyalis noon na baka hindi ipagpatuloy.

**[06:07:50.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009370)  
Sabi ni President Duterte nung 2016. Sabi niya, ipagpatuloy niya lahat.

**[06:07:56.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009371)  
Presidente na ako. Presidente ako ng buong Pilipinas.

**[06:08:00.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009372)  
Dapat pantay-pantay.

**[06:08:02.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009373)  
At pantay-pantay yung distribution ng mga proyekto.

**[06:08:05.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009374)  
From Region 1 to Region 13 hanggang BARM.

**[06:08:11.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009375)  
At siya mismo pinuntahan yan. Even sa Ilocos.

**[06:08:13.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009376)  
Sabi niya, gusto ko pantay-pantay yung mga proyekto.

**[06:08:17.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009377)  
Ang mga binisita natin noon. Sana po'y huwag lang po malimutan rin po ang Mindanao at ang Visayas rin po.

**[06:08:27.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009378)  
At sa BARM, salamat po. Ibig sabihin, huwag mo namang kalimutan doon.

**[06:08:32.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009379)  
In fact, Wins, ha?

**[06:08:33.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009380)  
Oo po. Makakaasa po kayo. In fact, tumaas po ang allocation ng Mindanao to 27.55% of the entire DPWH.

**[06:08:42.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009381)  
At bumaba po ang Luzon. Tumaas po ang Visayas to 20%.

**[06:08:47.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009382)  
At yung mga forest regions, lalo na po yung Region 5, Region 8, Region 13, nagtaasan din po.

**[06:08:55.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009383)  
So, we will strive po to make it more equitable.

**[06:08:58.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009384)  
And makakaasa po kayo. In fact po, next week nga po, papunta po ako ng Region 11 at Region 12, Senator.

**[06:09:06.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009385)  
At yung ating flood mitigation sa barangay sa Talomo sa Dabao.

**[06:09:12.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009386)  
Mukhang marapit na pong matapos yung ginawa natin after the big flood doon po.

**[06:09:19.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009387)  
At sisiguraduin po natin na maayos ang paggagawa at mabilis po natapos.

**[06:09:24.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009388)  
Salamat po, Sec. Wins.

**[06:09:26.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-009389)  
Punta ka pala doon? Ano, hindi ka naman nagasabi?

**[06:09:29.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-009390)
